{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01T14na4K1v68VDHUhMx9kZU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01SALkJStBXUyQiUX329XF6y","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/simpo-loss-implementation.md","content":"# SimPO Loss Implementation Guide\n\n## Overview\nSimPO (Simple Preference Optimization) is a preference optimization algorithm that trains language models using pairwise preferences without a reference model. This guide covers the mathematical formulation and PyTorch implementation of the SimPO loss.\n\n## Mathematical Foundation\n\n### Core Concept\nSimPO is designed to optimize a language model policy by comparing chosen (preferred) and rejected (dispreferred) responses. The loss function encourages higher rewards for chosen responses and lower rewards for rejected responses.\n\n### Key Components\n\n1. **Reward Model**: In SimPO, the reward is implicitly defined as:\n   - `r(x, y) = β * log(π(y|x) / π_ref(y|x))`\n   - Where π is the policy model and π_ref is a reference model\n   - However, SimPO removes the reference model dependency\n\n2. **Reference-Free Formulation**: SimPO simplifies the reward to:\n   - `r(x, y) = β * log π(y|x)` (log probability scaled by β)\n   - This is proportional to the model's log probability of generating the response\n\n3. **SimPO Loss Function**:\n   - The loss is based on a target margin between chosen and rejected rewards\n   - Formula: `L = -log(σ(β * (log π(y_w|x) - log π(y_l|x)) - γ))`\n   - Where:\n     - `y_w` is the chosen/winning response\n     - `y_l` is the rejected/losing response\n     - `β` (beta) is the reward scale parameter\n     - `γ` (gamma) is the target reward margin\n     - `σ` is the sigmoid function\n\n4. **Alternative Sigmoid-Based Formulation**:\n   - The loss can also be expressed as: `L = -log(sigmoid(β * (log π(y_w|x) - log π(y_l|x)) - γ))`\n\n## PyTorch Implementation Pattern\n\n```python\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\n\ndef simpo_loss(policy_chosen_logps, policy_rejected_logps, beta=2.0, gamma_beta_ratio=0.25):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute SimPO loss.\n\n    Args:\n        policy_chosen_logps: Log probabilities for chosen responses (batch_size,)\n        policy_rejected_logps: Log probabilities for rejected responses (batch_size,)\n        beta: Reward scale parameter\n        gamma_beta_ratio: Ratio of gamma to beta (gamma = gamma_beta_ratio * beta)\n\n    Returns:\n        losses: SimPO loss for each example\n        chosen_rewards: Rewards for chosen responses\n        rejected_rewards: Rewards for rejected responses\n    \"\"\"\n    # Calculate gamma from gamma_beta_ratio\n    gamma = gamma_beta_ratio * beta\n\n    # Calculate rewards (log probability scaled by beta)\n    chosen_rewards = beta * policy_chosen_logps\n    rejected_rewards = beta * policy_rejected_logps\n\n    # Calculate log probability difference\n    logps_diff = policy_chosen_logps - policy_rejected_logps\n\n    # SimPO loss with sigmoid: -log(sigmoid(β * (log_π(y_w) - log_π(y_l)) - γ))\n    # This is equivalent to: log(1 + exp(γ - β * (log_π(y_w) - log_π(y_l))))\n    losses = -F.logsigmoid(beta * logps_diff - gamma)\n\n    return losses, chosen_rewards, rejected_rewards\n```\n\n## Key Notes\n\n1. **Reward Scaling**: The beta parameter controls the scale of rewards derived from log probabilities\n2. **Target Margin**: Gamma defines the desired margin between chosen and rejected log probabilities\n3. **Sigmoid Loss**: The use of sigmoid ensures the loss is bounded and differentiable\n4. **Log Probability Difference**: The actual comparison is based on the log probability difference, not absolute values\n5. **Numerical Stability**: Using `F.logsigmoid` is numerically stable compared to computing log(sigmoid(...))\n\n## Common Parameter Settings\n\n- **beta**: Typically 2.0-10.0, with larger values in recent versions\n- **gamma_beta_ratio**: Typically 0.1-0.8, represents the target reward margin as a ratio of beta\n- **gamma**: Computed as `gamma_beta_ratio * beta`\n\n## References\n\n- SimPO paper: \"Simple Preference Optimization with a Reference-Free Reward\" (NEURIPS 2024)\n- Related: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), IPO, and other preference optimization methods\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":5,"cache_creation_input_tokens":113,"cache_read_input_tokens":47989,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":113,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"dd163b38-02fc-4113-920c-7bbab17a69fa","uuid":"e494562e-96ab-4a51-8cb4-06111ced4213"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_vrtx_01SALkJStBXUyQiUX329XF6y","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /root/environment/skills/simpo-loss-implementation.md"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"dd163b38-02fc-4113-920c-7bbab17a69fa","uuid":"ab0a9c9c-02d4-4ddd-954e-d8aa90307334","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/root/environment/skills/simpo-loss-implementation.md","content":"# SimPO Loss Implementation Guide\n\n## Overview\nSimPO (Simple Preference Optimization) is a preference optimization algorithm that trains language models using pairwise preferences without a reference model. This guide covers the mathematical formulation and PyTorch implementation of the SimPO loss.\n\n## Mathematical Foundation\n\n### Core Concept\nSimPO is designed to optimize a language model policy by comparing chosen (preferred) and rejected (dispreferred) responses. The loss function encourages higher rewards for chosen responses and lower rewards for rejected responses.\n\n### Key Components\n\n1. **Reward Model**: In SimPO, the reward is implicitly defined as:\n   - `r(x, y) = β * log(π(y|x) / π_ref(y|x))`\n   - Where π is the policy model and π_ref is a reference model\n   - However, SimPO removes the reference model dependency\n\n2. **Reference-Free Formulation**: SimPO simplifies the reward to:\n   - `r(x, y) = β * log π(y|x)` (log probability scaled by β)\n   - This is proportional to the model's log probability of generating the response\n\n3. **SimPO Loss Function**:\n   - The loss is based on a target margin between chosen and rejected rewards\n   - Formula: `L = -log(σ(β * (log π(y_w|x) - log π(y_l|x)) - γ))`\n   - Where:\n     - `y_w` is the chosen/winning response\n     - `y_l` is the rejected/losing response\n     - `β` (beta) is the reward scale parameter\n     - `γ` (gamma) is the target reward margin\n     - `σ` is the sigmoid function\n\n4. **Alternative Sigmoid-Based Formulation**:\n   - The loss can also be expressed as: `L = -log(sigmoid(β * (log π(y_w|x) - log π(y_l|x)) - γ))`\n\n## PyTorch Implementation Pattern\n\n```python\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\n\ndef simpo_loss(policy_chosen_logps, policy_rejected_logps, beta=2.0, gamma_beta_ratio=0.25):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute SimPO loss.\n\n    Args:\n        policy_chosen_logps: Log probabilities for chosen responses (batch_size,)\n        policy_rejected_logps: Log probabilities for rejected responses (batch_size,)\n        beta: Reward scale parameter\n        gamma_beta_ratio: Ratio of gamma to beta (gamma = gamma_beta_ratio * beta)\n\n    Returns:\n        losses: SimPO loss for each example\n        chosen_rewards: Rewards for chosen responses\n        rejected_rewards: Rewards for rejected responses\n    \"\"\"\n    # Calculate gamma from gamma_beta_ratio\n    gamma = gamma_beta_ratio * beta\n\n    # Calculate rewards (log probability scaled by beta)\n    chosen_rewards = beta * policy_chosen_logps\n    rejected_rewards = beta * policy_rejected_logps\n\n    # Calculate log probability difference\n    logps_diff = policy_chosen_logps - policy_rejected_logps\n\n    # SimPO loss with sigmoid: -log(sigmoid(β * (log_π(y_w) - log_π(y_l)) - γ))\n    # This is equivalent to: log(1 + exp(γ - β * (log_π(y_w) - log_π(y_l))))\n    losses = -F.logsigmoid(beta * logps_diff - gamma)\n\n    return losses, chosen_rewards, rejected_rewards\n```\n\n## Key Notes\n\n1. **Reward Scaling**: The beta parameter controls the scale of rewards derived from log probabilities\n2. **Target Margin**: Gamma defines the desired margin between chosen and rejected log probabilities\n3. **Sigmoid Loss**: The use of sigmoid ensures the loss is bounded and differentiable\n4. **Log Probability Difference**: The actual comparison is based on the log probability difference, not absolute values\n5. **Numerical Stability**: Using `F.logsigmoid` is numerically stable compared to computing log(sigmoid(...))\n\n## Common Parameter Settings\n\n- **beta**: Typically 2.0-10.0, with larger values in recent versions\n- **gamma_beta_ratio**: Typically 0.1-0.8, represents the target reward margin as a ratio of beta\n- **gamma**: Computed as `gamma_beta_ratio * beta`\n\n## References\n\n- SimPO paper: \"Simple Preference Optimization with a Reference-Free Reward\" (NEURIPS 2024)\n- Related: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), IPO, and other preference optimization methods\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

F.logsigmoid()\n- **Purpose**: Numerically stable computation of log(sigmoid(x))\n- **Usage**: `torch.nn.functional.logsigmoid(input)`\n- **Why use it**: Directly computing log(sigmoid(x)) can cause numerical overflow/underflow\n- **Example**:\n  ```python\n  import torch.nn.functional as F\n  x = torch.tensor([0.5, -0.5, 2.0])\n  result = F.logsigmoid(x)\n  # More stable than: torch.log(torch.sigmoid(x))\n  ```\n\n### 2. Sigmoid Function\n- **Standard**: `torch.sigmoid(x)` - outputs values in [0, 1]\n- **Formula**: σ(x) = 1 / (1 + exp(-x))\n- **Use case**: Converting scores to probabilities\n- **Example**:\n  ```python\n  scores = torch.randn(10)\n  probs = torch.sigmoid(scores)\n  ```\n\n### 3. Tensor Operations\n- **Element-wise operations**: Addition, subtraction, multiplication\n  ```python\n  a = torch.tensor([1.0, 2.0])\n  b = torch.tensor([3.0, 4.0])\n  c = a - b  # Element-wise subtraction\n  d = a * b  # Element-wise multiplication\n  ```\n\n### 4. Mean and Reduction\n- **Mean**: `tensor.mean()` - average across all elements\n- **Mean with dimension**: `tensor.mean(dim=0)` - average along specific dimension\n- **Sum**: `tensor.sum()` or `tensor.sum(dim=...)` for dimension-specific sums\n\n### 5. Device Management\n- **Move to device**: `tensor.to(device)` or `tensor.cuda()`, `tensor.cpu()`\n- **Check device**: `tensor.device`\n- **Get device from another tensor**: `other_tensor.device`\n\n### 6. Gradient Operations\n- **Detach**: `tensor.detach()` - detach from computation graph\n- **Disable gradients**: `with torch.no_grad():`\n- **Clone with history**: `tensor.clone()`\n\n## Common Loss Calculation Patterns\n\n### Binary Classification Loss (Sigmoid)\n```python\nlogits = torch.randn(32)\ntargets = torch.randint(0, 2, (32,)).float()\nloss = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(logits, targets)\n```\n\n### Preference-based Loss (SimPO-style)\n```python\n# Calculate probability difference\nchosen_logps = torch.randn(32)  # log probs for chosen\nrejected_logps = torch.randn(32)  # log probs for rejected\nbeta = 2.0\ngamma = 0.5\n\n# SimPO loss\nlogps_diff = chosen_logps - rejected_logps\nlosses = -F.logsigmoid(beta * logps_diff - gamma)\nloss = losses.mean()\n```\n\n## Tensor Shape Management\n\n### Ensuring Compatibility\n- **Add dimension**: `tensor.unsqueeze(dim)`\n- **Remove dimension**: `tensor.squeeze()` or `tensor.squeeze(dim)`\n- **Reshape**: `tensor.reshape(shape)` or `tensor.view(shape)`\n- **Transpose**: `tensor.transpose(dim0, dim1)` or `tensor.permute(...)`\n\n## Numerical Stability Tips\n\n1. **Use functional versions**: `F.logsigmoid()` instead of `torch.log(torch.sigmoid())`\n2. **Use log-space operations**: When dealing with probabilities, work in log space\n3. **Avoid large exponentials**: Use softmax from logits rather than normalizing probabilities\n4. **Batch normalization**: Helps stabilize training\n\n## Performance Considerations\n\n1. **Device placement**: Keep tensors on the same device (CPU or GPU)\n2. **Data types**: Use float32 by default, float16 for memory efficiency (with care)\n3. **In-place operations**: Use cautiously in training (avoid with autograd)\n4. **Batch operations**: Vectorize operations when possible\n\n## Common Debug Patterns\n\n```python\n# Check tensor properties\nprint(tensor.shape)\nprint(tensor.dtype)\nprint(tensor.device)\n\n# Check for NaN/Inf\nassert torch.isfinite(tensor).all()\nprint(f\"Min: {tensor.min()}, Max: {tensor.max()}\")\n\n# Check gradients\nprint(f\"Requires grad: {tensor.requires_grad}\")\n```\n\n## References\n- PyTorch documentation: https://pytorch.org/docs/\n- Numerical stability guide: https://discuss.pytorch.org/t/numerical-stability/\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1224,"cache_read_input_tokens":48102,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1224,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"dd163b38-02fc-4113-920c-7bbab17a69fa","uuid":"f9abb204-f99c-4887-9b98-2f436d17956b"}

Shape: (batch_size,)\n            policy_rejected_logps: Log probabilities of the policy model for the rejected responses. Shape: (batch_size,)\n\n        Returns:\n            A tuple of three tensors: (losses, chosen_rewards, rejected_rewards).\n            The losses tensor contains the SimPO loss for each example in the batch.\n            The chosen_rewards and rejected_rewards tensors contain the rewards for the chosen and rejected responses, respectively.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Calculate gamma from gamma_beta_ratio\n        gamma = self.gamma_beta_ratio * self.beta\n\n        # Calculate rewards as scaled log probabilities\n        chosen_rewards = self.beta * policy_chosen_logps\n        rejected_rewards = self.beta * policy_rejected_logps\n\n        # Calculate log probability difference\n        logps_diff = policy_chosen_logps - policy_rejected_logps\n\n        # SimPO loss: -log(sigmoid(β * (log_π(y_w) - log_π(y_l)) - γ))\n        # This is equivalent to: log(1 + exp(γ - β * (log_π(y_w) - log_π(y_l))))\n        # Using F.logsigmoid for numerical stability: logsigmoid(x) = log(1 / (1 + exp(-x)))\n        # So: -logsigmoid(x) = -log(1 / (1 + exp(-x))) = log(1 + exp(-x))\n        # And: -logsigmoid(β * logps_diff - γ) = log(1 + exp(-(β * logps_diff - γ)))\n        #                                       = log(1 + exp(γ - β * logps_diff))\n        losses = -F.logsigmoid(self.beta * logps_diff - gamma)\n\n        return losses, chosen_rewards, rejected_rewards"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1558,"cache_read_input_tokens":56548,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1558,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"dd163b38-02fc-4113-920c-7bbab17a69fa","uuid":"6a3c025a-3f11-4161-9700-205de024c84a"}

                                      = log(1 + exp(γ - β * logps_diff))\n        losses = -F.logsigmoid(self.beta * logps_diff - gamma)\n\n        return losses, chosen_rewards, rejected_rewards","originalFile":"\nimport inspect\nimport random\nimport warnings\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom contextlib import nullcontext\nfrom functools import wraps\nfrom typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Literal, Optional, Tuple, Union\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\nfrom accelerate import PartialState\nfrom datasets import Dataset\nfrom torch.utils.data import DataLoader\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, DataCollator, PreTrainedModel, PreTrainedTokenizerBase, Trainer\nfrom trl.trainer import CPOTrainer\nfrom transformers.trainer_callback import TrainerCallback\nfrom transformers.trainer_utils import EvalLoopOutput\nfrom transformers.utils import is_torch_fx_proxy\n\nfrom trl.import_utils import is_peft_available, is_wandb_available\nfrom scripts.simpo_config import SimPOConfig\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Dict, Literal, Optional\n\nfrom transformers import TrainingArguments\n\nfrom trl.trainer.utils import (\n    DPODataCollatorWithPadding,\n    disable_dropout_in_model,\n    pad_to_length,\n    peft_module_casting_to_bf16,\n    trl_sanitze_kwargs_for_tagging,\n)\n\nif is_peft_available():\n    from peft import PeftModel, get_peft_model, prepare_model_for_kbit_training\n\nif is_wandb_available():\n    import wandb\n\n\nclass SimPOTrainer(Trainer):\n    r\"\"\"\n    Initialize SimPOTrainer.\n\n    Args:\n        model (`transformers.PreTrainedModel`):\n            The model to train, preferably an `AutoModelForSequenceClassification`.\n        args (`SimPOConfig`):\n            The SimPO config arguments to use for training.\n        data_collator (`transformers.DataCollator`):\n            The data collator to use for training. First\n            we truncate the prompt; if we're still too long, we truncate the chosen/rejected.\n\n        We also create the labels for the chosen/rejected responses, which are of length equal to\n            the sum of the length of the prompt and the chosen/rejected response, with\n            label_pad_token_id  for the prompt tokens.\n        \"\"\"\n        batch = {}\n        prompt = feature[\"prompt\"]\n        chosen = feature[\"chosen\"]\n        rejected = feature[\"rejected\"]\n\n        if not self.is_encoder_decoder:\n            # Last prompt token might get merged by tokenizer and\n            # it should not be included for generation if that happens\n            prompt_len_input_ids = len(prompt_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"])\n\n            chosen_prompt_len_input_ids = len(chosen_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"])\n            rejected_prompt_len_input_ids = len(rejected_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"])\n            prompt_len_input_ids = min(chosen_prompt_len_input_ids, rejected_prompt_len_input_ids)\n\n            for k, v in prompt_tokens.items():\n                prompt_tokens[k] = v[:prompt_len_input_ids]\n\n            # Make sure prompts only have one different token at most an\n            # and length only differs by 1 at most\n            num_diff_tokens = sum(\n                [a != b for a, b in zip(chosen_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"], rejected_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"])]\n            )\n            num_diff_len = abs(chosen_prompt_len_input_ids - rejected_prompt_len_input_ids)\n            if num_diff_tokens > 1 or num_diff_len > 1:\n                raise ValueError(\n                    \"Chosen and rejected prompt_input_ids might only differ on the \"\n                    \"last token due to tokenizer merge ops.\"\n                )\n\n            # Avoid adding if it's already there\n            bos_token_id = self.tokenizer.bos_token_id\n            if prompt_len_input_ids == 0 or bos_token_id != prompt_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"][0]:\n                prompt_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"] = [bos_token_id] + prompt_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"]\n                prompt_tokens[\"prompt_attention_mask\"] = [1] + prompt_tokens[\"prompt_attention_mask\"]\n            if chosen_prompt_len_input_ids == 0 or bos_token_id != chosen_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"][0]:\n                chosen_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"] = [bos_token_id] + chosen_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"]\n                chosen_tokens[\"prompt_attention_mask\"] = [1] + chosen_tokens[\"prompt_attention_mask\"]\n            if rejected_prompt_len_input_ids == 0 or bos_token_id != rejected_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"][0]:\n                rejected_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"] = [bos_token_id] + rejected_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"]\n                rejected_tokens[\"prompt_attention_mask\"] = [1] + rejected_tokens[\"prompt_attention_mask\"]\n\n            # add EOS token to end of answer. Avoid adding if it's already there\n            eos_token_id = self.tokenizer.eos_token_id\n            if len(chosen_tokens[\"input_ids\"]) == 0 or eos_token_id != chosen_tokens[\"input_ids\"][-1]:\n self.max_prompt_length]\n                    elif self.truncation_mode == \"keep_end\":\n                        for k in [\"prompt_input_ids\", \"prompt_attention_mask\"]:\n                            answer_tokens[k] = answer_tokens[k][-self.max_prompt_length :]\n                    else:\n                        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown truncation mode: {self.truncation_mode}\")\n\n longer_response_length > self.max_length:\n                    for k in [\"input_ids\", \"attention_mask\"]:\n                        answer_tokens[k] = answer_tokens[k][: self.max_length -              self.label_pad_token_id\n            ] * len(chosen_tokens[\"prompt_input_ids\"])\n            rejected_sequence_tokens[\"labels\"] = rejected_sequence_tokens[\"input_ids\"][:]\n            for type_key, tokens in toks.items():\n                    if type_key == \"token_type_ids\":\n                        continue\n                    batch[f\"{k}{type_key}\"] = tokens\n\n concatenated_inputs(\n        batch: Dict[str, Union[List, torch.LongTensor]],\n        is_encoder_decoder: bool = False,\n        label_pad_token_id: int = -100,\n        padding_value: int = 0,\n        device: Optional[torch.device] = None,\n    ) -> Dict[str, torch.LongTensor]:\n        \"\"\"Concatenate the chosen and rejected inputs into a single tensor.\n\n        Args:\n            batch: A batch of data. Must contain the keys 'chosen_input_ids' and 'rejected_input_ids', which are tensors of shape (batch_size, sequence_length).\n            is_encoder_decoder: Whether the model is an encoder-decoder model.\n            label_pad_token_id: The label pad token id.\n            padding_value: The padding value to use for the concatenated inputs_ids.\n            device: The device for the concatenated_batch[concatenated_key] = pad_to_length(batch[k], max_length, pad_value=pad_value)\n        for k in batch:\n            if k.startswith(\"rejected\") and isinstance(batch[k], torch.Tensor):\n                if \"labels\" in k or is_encoder_decoder:\n                    pad_value = label_pad_token_id\n                elif k.endswith(\"_input_ids\"):\n                    pad_value = padding_value\n                elif k.endswith(\"_attention_mask\"):\n                    pad_value = 0\n                concatenated_key = k.replace(\"rejected\", \"concatenated\")\n batch[\"prompt_attention_mask\"].repeat(2, 1).to(device=device)\n            )\n\n        return concatenated_batch\n\n    def simpo_loss(\n        self,\n        policy_chosen_logps: policy model log probabilities.\n\n        Args:\n            policy_chosen_logps: Log probabilities of the policy model for the chosen responses. Shape: (batch_size,)\n            policy_rejected_logps: Log probabilities of the policy model for the rejected responses. torch.FloatTensor, torch.FloatTensor]:\n        \"\"\"Run the given model on the given batch of inputs, concatenating the chosen and rejected inputs together.\n\n        We do this to avoid doing two forward passes, because it's faster for FSDP.\n        \"\"\"\n        concatenated_batch = self.concatenated_inputs(\n            batch,\n            is_encoder_decoder=self.is_encoder_decoder,\n concatenated_batch[\"concatenated_labels\"][:len_chosen]\n\n        return (chosen_logps, rejected_logps, chosen_logits, rejected_logits, chosen_labels)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def get_batch_logps(\n        logits: torch.FloatTensor,\n        labels: torch.LongTensor,\n        average_log_prob: bool = True,\n        label_pad_token_id: int = -100,\n        is_encoder_decoder: bool = False,\n    ) -> torch.FloatTensor:\n        \"\"\"Compute the log probabilities of the given labels under the given logits.\n\n        Args:\n            logits: Logits of the model (unnormalized). Shape: (batch_size, sequence_length, vocab_size)\n            labels: Labels for which to compute the log probabilities. Label tokens with a value of label_pad_token_id are ignored. Shape: (batch_size, sequence_length)\n            average_log_prob: If True, return the average log probability per (non-masked) token. Otherwise, return the sum of the log probabilities of the (non-masked) tokens.\n            label_pad_token_id: The label pad token id.\n            is_encoder_decoder: Whether the model is an Shape: (batch_size,)\n        policy_rejected_logps: Log probabilities of the policy model for the rejected responses. Shape: (batch_size,)\n        beta: Reward scale parameter\n        gamma_beta_ratio: Ratio of target reward margin to beta\n\n    Returns:\n        A tuple of three tensors: (losses, chosen_rewards, rejected_rewards).\n    \"\"\"\n    # Shape: (batch_size,)\n            policy_rejected_logps: Log probabilities of the policy model for the rejected responses. Shape: (batch_size,)\n\n        Returns:\n            A tuple of three tensors: (losses, chosen_rewards, rejected_rewards).\n            The losses tensor contains the SimPO loss for each example in the batch.\n            The chosen_rewards and rejected_rewards tensors contain the rewards for the chosen and rejected responses, respectively.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Calculate gamma from gamma_beta_ratio\n        gamma = self.gamma_beta_ratio * self.beta\n\n        # Calculate rewards as scaled log probabilities\n        chosen_rewards = self.beta * policy_chosen_logps\n        rejected_rewards = self.beta * policy_rejected_logps\n\n        # Calculate log probability difference\n        logps_diff = policy_chosen_logps - policy_rejected_logps\n\n        # SimPO loss: -log(sigmoid(β * (log_π(y_w) - log_π(y_l)) - γ))\n        # This is equivalent to: log(1 + exp(γ - β * (log_π(y_w) - log_π(y_l))))\n        # Using F.logsigmoid for numerical stability: logsigmoid(x) = log(1 / (1 + exp(-x)))\n        # So: -logsigmoid(x) = -log(1 / (1 + exp(-x))) = log(1 + exp(-x))\n        # And: -logsigmoid(β * logps_diff - γ) = log(1 + exp(-(β * logps_diff - γ)))\n        #                                       = log(1 + exp(γ - β * logps_diff))\n        losses = -F.logsigmoid(self.beta * logps_diff - gamma)\n\n        return losses, chosen_rewards, rejected_rewards\n\n    def concatenated_forward(\n        self, model: nn.Module, batch: Dict[str, Union[List, torch.LongTensor]]\n    ) -> Tuple[torch.FloatTensor, torch.FloatTensor, torch.FloatTensor, torch.FloatTensor]:\n        \"\"\"Run the given model on the given batch of inputs, concatenating the chosen and rejected inputs together.","numLines":40,"startLine":560,"totalLines":893}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01DzVrLzXiogrXpqs4ViNeZu","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01VigiBGUPqgnCVu68aUmYnv","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/IMPLEMENTATION_SUMMARY.md","content":"# SimPO Loss Implementation Summary\n\n## Overview\nSuccessfully implemented the `simpo_loss` function for the `SimPOTrainer` class based on the SimPO paper's loss formulation.\n\n## Implementation Details\n\n### Function Location\n- **File**: `/root/SimPO/scripts/simpo_trainer.py`\n- **Method**: `SimPOTrainer.simpo_loss()` (lines 560-594)\n\n### Mathematical Foundation\n\nThe SimPO loss is based on the following formula:\n\n```\nL = -log(sigmoid(β * (log π(y_w|x) - log π(y_l|x)) - γ))\n```\n\nWhere:\n- `y_w`: Chosen/winning response\n- `y_l`: Rejected/losing response\n- `β` (beta): Reward scale parameter (from `self.beta`)\n- `γ` (gamma): Target reward margin = `gamma_beta_ratio * beta`\n- `log π(y|x)`: Log probability of generating response from policy model\n\n### Implementation Features\n\n1. **Numerical Stability**: Uses `F.logsigmoid()` instead of `log(sigmoid())` to avoid numerical overflow/underflow\n\n2. **Reward Calculation**:\n   - Chosen reward = `β * log π(y_w|x)`\n   - Rejected reward = `β * log π(y_l|x)`\n\n3. **Loss Computation**:\n   - Computes log probability difference\n   - Applies sigmoid-based loss with target margin\n   - Returns tuple of (losses, chosen_rewards, rejected_rewards)\n\n### Code Implementation\n\n```python\ndef simpo_loss(\n    self,\n    policy_chosen_logps: torch.FloatTensor,\n    policy_rejected_logps: torch.FloatTensor,\n) -> Tuple[torch.FloatTensor, torch.FloatTensor, torch.FloatTensor]:\n    \"\"\"Compute the SimPO loss for a batch of policy model log probabilities.\"\"\"\n\n    # Calculate gamma from gamma_beta_ratio\n    gamma = self.gamma_beta_ratio * self.beta\n\n    # Calculate rewards as scaled log probabilities\n    chosen_rewards = self.beta * policy_chosen_logps\n    rejected_rewards = self.beta * policy_rejected_logps\n\n    # Calculate log probability difference\n    logps_diff = policy_chosen_logps - policy_rejected_logps\n\n    # SimPO loss with numerical stability\n    losses = -F.logsigmoid(self.beta * logps_diff - gamma)\n\n    return losses, chosen_rewards, rejected_rewards\n```\n\n## Testing Results\n\n### Test Configuration\n- Input: 100 log probability pairs (chosen and rejected responses)\n- Default parameters: beta=2.0, gamma_beta_ratio=0.25\n\n### Output\n- **Loss file**: `/root/loss.npz`\n- **Key**: `'losses'`\n- **Shape**: (100,)\n- **Data type**: float32\n\n### Statistics\n- **Mean loss**: 0.164137\n- **Std dev**: 0.238839\n- **Min loss**: 0.003439\n- **Max loss**: 1.610444\n\n### Sample Values (first 10)\n```\n[0.13187213, 0.16114417, 0.10623718, 0.1144174, 0.3773888,\n 0.042091, 0.08354614, 0.10378353, 0.07619784, 0.01080376]\n```\n\n## Environment Setup\n\n### Python Version\n```\nPython 3.12.3 (main, Jan  8 2026, 11:30:50) [GCC 13.3.0]\n```\n\n### Key Dependencies\n- `torch==2.10.0+cu128`\n- `transformers==5.0.0`\n- `numpy==2.4.1`\n- `datasets==4.5.0`\n- `trl==0.27.1`\n- `accelerate==1.12.0`\n\n### Environment Info\n- Full pip freeze output saved to: `/root/python_info.txt`\n\n## Verification\n\n✓ Implementation complete in trainer file\n✓ Loss function tested with fixed input tensors\n✓ Results saved to `/root/loss.npz` with key `'losses'`\n✓ Results reproducible and verified\n✓ Environment information logged\n\n## Files Generated\n\n1. **Implementation**: `/root/SimPO/scripts/simpo_trainer.py` (modified)\n2. **Loss Results**: `/root/loss.npz` (100 loss values)\n3. **Environment Info**: `/root/python_info.txt` (Python version + pip freeze)\n4. **Skills Documentation**:\n   - `/root/SimPO/test_simpo_loss.py`\n   - `/root/SimPO/run_unit_test.py`\n\n## Key Implementation Notes\n\n1. **No Reference Model**: Unlike DPO, SimPO doesn't use a reference model. The reward is purely based on policy log probabilities scaled by beta.\n\n2. **Target Margin**: The gamma (target reward margin) is specified as a ratio to beta, making it easier to tune across different beta values.\n\n3. **Sigmoid-Based Loss**: The loss encourages the margin between chosen and rejected log probabilities to exceed gamma while using a smooth sigmoid function.\n\n4. **Integration**: The function integrates seamlessly with the rest of the SimPOTrainer, which uses it in:\n   - `get_batch_loss_metrics()` for training/evaluation\n   - `compute_loss()` for the main training loop\n   - Metrics computation for reward tracking\n\n## References\n\n- SimPO Paper: \"Simple Preference Optimization with a Reference-Free Reward\" (NeurIPS 2024)\n- Related Work: DPO (Direct Preference Optimization), IPO, and other preference optimization methods\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":997,"cache_read_input_tokens":68690,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":997,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"dd163b38-02fc-4113-920c-7bbab17a69fa","uuid":"7081b3ca-2c55-4de5-89e5-aea1c61333a0"}
